Tasks never have Lua. `via` tags: **beh** = task/role stage `<behavior>`, **link** = skill `<link target>`, **call** = `<call query>`, **rsk** = role `<skills>`, **res** = skill `<resources>`, **meta** = task `spark.metadata-class`, **str** = XML `<string>` value (commander mission lists: these hold mission dia-types, matched here only when equal to a task name),**lua** = dotted name in Lua (string or bare; comments stripped), **ext/impl/type** = `<extends>` / `<implements>` / `<object type>`. Task ✗ = no task with that name in this model's dia5 (e.g. `taskgroup.*` lives in the `taskgroups` submodule). Params = non-context `dia-name`s. Roles = `dia-type` of the `Models.xml` models offering the mission. Frag orders = `<fragorder>` children of this mission in those models, with the number of models listing each (model-level frag orders available without a mission are not included). Many frag orders start no task and are polled by skills instead (see THEMES gotchas).

### Frag orders in `physical/mildb/Missions.xml`

Init = initializer in `FragOrder.lua` `fragOrderTaskInitializers` (no initializer → `integration.startFragOrder`, handled by the kernel / integration layer). Starts = task(s) the initializer starts (`return true, "…"`, or the dia-type itself on a bare `return true`). Prop = listed in `fragOrdersToBePropagated`. Auto = tested in `fragOrder.executeAutomatFragOrder`.

## 7.worldwide-tests Missions in `physical/worldwide-tests`

Task ✗ = no task with that name in this model's dia5 (e.g. `taskgroup.*` lives in the `taskgroups` submodule). Params = non-context `dia-name`s. Roles = `dia-type` of the `Models.xml` models offering the mission. Frag orders = `<fragorder>` children of this mission in those models, with the number of models listing each (model-level frag orders available without a mission are not included). Many frag orders start no task and are polled by skills instead (see THEMES gotchas).

| DB | Mission | Section | id | Behavior → task | dia-type (if different) | Params | Models | Roles | Frag orders |

### Frag orders in `physical/worldwide-tests/Missions.xml`

Init = initializer in `FragOrder.lua` `fragOrderTaskInitializers` (no initializer → `integration.startFragOrder`, handled by the kernel / integration layer). Starts = task(s) the initializer starts (`return true, "…"`, or the dia-type itself on a bare `return true`). Prop = listed in `fragOrdersToBePropagated`. Auto = tested in `fragOrder.executeAutomatFragOrder`.
